# Imported Westwood libraries

Generated by `tools/vc6/report.py` from the ledger `docs/data/imports`. Each library was imported from EA's published copies (R Renegade, G Generals, Z Zero Hour; commits Renegade `CnC_Renegade@3e00c3a`, Generals `CnC_Generals_Zero_Hour@0a05454`, Zero Hour `CnC_Generals_Zero_Hour@0a05454`), file by file, keeping the copy whose compiled code matches client.exe best, then splicing in whole function definitions from other copies where that adds byte-exact functions without losing any. "In tree": client functions our own source reproduces byte for byte (compiled with VC6 SP4, `/MT /W3 /O2 /Ob2`). See docs/library-matching.md Â§3.5.

## Summary

"Exact, ambiguous address" (D54): functions whose compiled bytes occur at several places in client.exe, all copies identical (relocated fields included), so no single address can be credited; counted once per client function, never as pairs.

| Library | Base copy | Source files | From another copy | Functions merged | In tree | Exact, ambiguous address | Best single copy |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| app | reconstructed | 594 | 0 | 0 | 14875 | 112 | - |
| binkmovie | Renegade | 4 | 0 | 0 | 59 | 1 | 21 |
| wlib | Zero Hour | 8 | 1 | 0 | 146 | 5 | 74 |
| wnet | Zero Hour | 4 | 3 | 0 | 67 | 0 | 7 |
| ww3d2 | Renegade | 108 | 32 | 11 | 3757 | 33 | 1323 |
| wwaudio | Renegade | 19 | 0 | 0 | 784 | 2 | 205 |
| wwdebug | Zero Hour | 3 | 0 | 0 | 114 | 0 | 65 |
| wwlib | Renegade | 74 | 4 | 1 | 782 | 32 | 535 |
| wwmath | Renegade | 35 | 2 | 0 | 541 | 10 | 366 |
| wwsaveload | Zero Hour | 12 | 0 | 0 | 170 | 8 | 75 |
| wwshade | Zero Hour | 22 | 0 | 0 | 487 | 0 | 62 |
